Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The chromatography variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 932 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 321 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_932(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 932. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^321(S, F) is trivial. QED.
